HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_08fc4c839a2861b130e2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:2:2\",\"root_000059\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"İnsanın gözbebeğindeki küçük görüntü çağrışımı, kendini gören kişinin kendi görüntüsünü bütün gerçeklik sanması ihtimalini açar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0fd210977a3a979b93a4","text":"3. **The human as a perceiving image who can misread himself (exploratory).** `ء ن س:B002` supplies perception entering awareness, and `ء ن س:B005` the small human image visible in the pupil. In 96:5–7, `الإنسان` repeats before the reflexive `رآه`: the human learns, then sees himself as sufficient. The eye-image branch acts as a mirror model in which a visible image is mistaken for the whole perceiving self. I infer, more tentatively, that the lexical eye-image sharpens the reflexive grammar. The fixed `الإنسان` can thus carry an ironic vulnerability: the perceiver may absolutize his own image.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001","root_001039/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1217bd5011c0df14e9f0","text":"2. **Formation as a passage from attachment into proportioned presence.** `خ ل ق:B001` supplies measuring, `خ ل ق:B002` bringing into being, and `خ ل ق:B003` complete, well-proportioned form; `ع ل ق:B003` supplies congealed blood and a blood-sucking attached body. The sequence continues from this compact attached material to instruction by pen and acquired knowledge (96:4–5). I infer that the juxtaposed stages describe one formative trajectory. `خلق` then highlights transformation and proportioning, while `علق` keeps the materially dependent starting condition visible inside the finished human.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_000434/B001","root_000434/B003","root_001039/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2e92d5c5c6935124e92c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:2:1\",\"root_000434\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]],[\"96:2:2\",\"root_000059\",[\"B001\"]],[\"96:2:4\",\"root_001039\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İnsan, gizli ve yapışık bir maddeden ölçülü, görünür bir varlığa geçen oluşum süreci içinde gösterilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001039/B001","root_001039/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_50dd32518d27adbe6212","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:2:4\",\"root_001039\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sonraki perçem sahnesi, gizli oluşumda tutunarak başlayan bedenin kamusal alanda da denetlenebilir ve savunmasız kaldığını geriye dönük olarak açığa çıkarır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_692ed4b145d8d5a6a950","text":"96:7 supplied the decisive retrospective reversal: the being made from clinging `علق` judges himself independent. Its reflexive `رآه` also made the pupil-image branch `ء ن س:B005` visible, while 96:4–5 confirmed that attachment opens into a teachable developmental sequence. The later context therefore preserved dependence as a present diagnosis rather than a superseded biological stage.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7b69aa67026e01f295d2","text":"The later forelock scene made `عَلَقٍ` surprisingly bodily again: the body first clings in hidden formation, then the forelock is seized in public exposure. The same vulnerable embodiment that begins life becomes the point where false authority is checked.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_000434/B002","root_001039/B001","root_001039/B003","root_001039/B006","root_001039/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ab966513eb411e53ac95","text":"1. The human is read as visible presence produced from hidden attachment. Evidence: `ء ن س B001` visible human presence over against hidden/wild beings; `ع ل ق B003` clotted blood/leech-like attachment; `ع ل ق B009` conception taking hold; `خ ل ق B002` bringing into being. Structural cue: the first named created object is `ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ`, and its source is a singular indefinite `عَلَقٍ`. Mechanism: public human presence is grounded in a small clinging, blood-like, womb-bound origin. Abductive move: the packet supplies the biological/clinging branches; I infer that the ayah compresses visibility from invisibility and autonomy from dependence. Reading change: “human” no longer reads as an independent subject but as a dependent emergence from attachment. 2. `عَلَقٍ` activates not only embryo/clot but also clinging claim and snare. Evidence: `ع ل ق B001` clinging/hanging, `ع ل ق B005` quarrel or claim fastening to someone, `ع ل ق B011` game caught in a snare, and later 96:9-10 the one who restrains a praying servant. Structural cue: after the origin in clinging, the packet later shows a social actor clinging to another's worship by obstruction. Mechanism: attachment can be life-giving origin or coercive entanglement. Abductive move: the packet supplies a root with biological and legal/social branches; I infer that later obstruction reactivates the negative side of attachment. Reading change: the ayah becomes the seed of the whole conflict: the creature born by attachment later misuses attachment as control. 3. The “from clinging” origin undercuts the self-sufficiency of 96:7. Evidence: `ع ل ق B006` meager subsistence to get by and `غ ن ي B001/B002` self-sufficiency/sufficing. Structural cue: 96:2 precedes 96:7 in the same human sequence: created from dependence, then sees himself independent. Mechanism: the later claim of sufficiency is exposed by the earlier material origin. Abductive move: the packet gives origin and later self-assessment; I infer polemical contrast. Reading change: 96:2 becomes an anti-arrogance premise, not only an anthropology statement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001039/B001","root_001039/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c8d6cdd4f6bc8dea95fd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:2:4\",\"root_001039\",[\"B001\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yapışarak tutunan başlangıç, geride kalmış bir biyolojik evre değil, insanın kendini yeterli sanmasını sürekli yalanlayan bağımlılık yapısıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001039/B001","root_001039/B005","root_001039/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ce8884eb9de9e982412f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:2:4\",\"root_001039\",[\"B001\",\"B005\",\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Hayat veren yapışma, ileride namaz kılanı engelleyen kişinin başkasına ilişip onu denetleme çabasına dönüşen olumsuz bir bağlanma ihtimalini de taşır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001039/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f27ffca95d184c9aa7c2","text":"1. **Constitutive dependence rather than a discarded first stage.** `ع ل ق:B001` supplies something clinging to or hanging from another, and `ع ل ق:B009` supplies conception taking hold. The same human is taught in 96:5, crosses bounds in 96:6, and sees himself as self-sufficient in 96:7 (`غ ن ي:B001`). The attachment branch functions as the human’s dependency architecture; the later self-assessment is its exact reversal. I infer that origin remains diagnostic of present status rather than becoming irrelevant after growth. The ayah therefore reads as a standing contradiction of imagined independence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
